=== FILE: C:\Users\priya\OneDrive\Desktop\COHORT\WEB DEV\PROJECT\UP-PET-Exam\src\App.tsx ===
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Navigate, Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";
import { AppHeader } from "./components/AppHeader";
import { loadLibrary, type LibrarySnapshot } from "./data/examLibrary";
import { loadPreferences, savePreferences } from "./data/persistence";
import { ExamPage } from "./pages/ExamPage";
import { HistoryPage } from "./pages/HistoryPage";
import { InstructionsPage } from "./pages/InstructionsPage";
import { LibraryPage } from "./pages/LibraryPage";
import { ManagePage } from "./pages/ManagePage";
import { ResultsPage } from "./pages/ResultsPage";
import type { UserPreferences } from "./types/exam";

export default function App() {
  const [prefs, setPrefs] = useState<UserPreferences>(loadPreferences);
  const [library, setLibrary] = useState<LibrarySnapshot | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const refresh = () => {
    loadLibrary()
      .then(setLibrary)
      .catch((err: unknown) => setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load exam registry."));
  };

  useEffect(() => { refresh(); }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    savePreferences(prefs);
    document.documentElement.dataset.theme = prefs.theme;
  }, [prefs]);

  return (
    <>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/exam/:examId" element={<ExamPage />} />
        <Route
          path="*"
          element={
            <>
              <AppHeader prefs={prefs} onPrefs={setPrefs} />
              {error ? <p className="notice">{error}</p> : null}
              {!library ? <p className="page">Loading registry…</p> : (
                <Routes>
                  <Route path="/" element={<LibraryPage library={library} />} />
                  <Route path="/history" element={<HistoryPage />} />
                  <Route path="/manage" element={<ManagePage library={library} onChange={refresh} />} />
                  <Route path="/exam/:examId/instructions" element={<InstructionsPage />} />
                  <Route path="/results/:attemptId" element={<ResultsPage />} />
                  <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
                </Routes>
              )}
            </>
          }
        />
      </Routes>
    </>
  );
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\priya\OneDrive\Desktop\COHORT\WEB DEV\PROJECT\UP-PET-Exam\src\counter.ts ===
export function setupCounter(element: HTMLButtonElement) {
  let counter = 0
  const setCounter = (count: number) => {
    counter = count
    element.innerHTML = `Count is ${counter}`
  }
  element.addEventListener('click', () => setCounter(counter + 1))
  setCounter(0)
}

import type { ExamContext, LanguageMode } from "../types/exam";
import { MathText, pickText } from "./MathText";

export function ContextBlock({
  context,
  mode,
}: {
  context: ExamContext;
  mode: LanguageMode;
}) {
  const langs: Array<"en" | "hi"> = mode === "bilingual" ? ["hi", "en"] : [mode];
  return (
    <section className="context" aria-label="Question context">
      {langs.map((lang) => {
        const directions = pickText(context.directions, lang);
        const title = pickText(context.title ?? undefined, lang);
        const passage = pickText(context.passage ?? undefined, lang);
        return (
          <div key={lang} className="lang-block">
            {mode === "bilingual" ? <div className="lang-label">{lang === "hi" ? "हिन्दी" : "English"}</div> : null}
            {directions ? <p><MathText text={directions} /></p> : null}
            {title ? <h3>{title}</h3> : null}
            {passage ? <p className="q-text"><MathText text={passage} /></p> : null}
          </div>
        );
      })}
      {context.table ? <ExamTableView table={context.table} mode={mode} /> : null}
      {context.chart ? <ExamChartView chart={context.chart} mode={mode} type={context.type} /> : null}
    </section>
  );
}

function ExamTableView({
  table,
  mode,
}: {
  table: NonNullable<ExamContext["table"]>;
  mode: LanguageMode;
}) {
  const lang = mode === "en" ? "en" : "hi";
  return (
    <div className="chart">
      <table className="data-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>{pickText(table.rowHeader, lang) || (mode === "hi" ? "मद" : "Item")}</th>
            {table.columns.map((column, index) => (
              <th key={index}>{pickText(column, lang)}</th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {table.rows.map((row, index) => (
            <tr key={index}>
              <td>{pickText(typeof row.label === "string" ? { en: row.label, hi: row.label } : row.label, lang)}</td>
              {row.values.map((value, valueIndex) => (
                <td key={valueIndex}>{String(value)}</td>
              ))}
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

function ExamChartView({
  chart,
  mode,
  type,
}: {
  chart: NonNullable<ExamContext["chart"]>;
  mode: LanguageMode;
  type: string;
}) {
  const lang = mode === "en" ? "en" : "hi";
  const max = Math.max(...chart.series.flatMap((series) => series.values), 1);
  return (
    <div className="chart" role="img" aria-label={type === "line_chart" ? "Line chart data" : "Bar chart data"}>
      {chart.series.map((series) => (
        <div key={pickText(series.name, lang)} className="bars" style={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>
          <strong>{pickText(series.name, lang)}{chart.unit ? ` (${chart.unit})` : ""}</strong>
          {chart.categories.map((category, index) => {
            const value = series.values[index] ?? 0;
            return (
              <div className="bar-row" key={`${category}-${index}`}>
                <span>{category}</span>
                <div className="bar" style={{ width: `${Math.max(4, (value / max) * 100)}%` }} />
                <span>{value}</span>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ))}
      {chart.note ? <p className="muted">{chart.note}</p> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode } from "react";

export function Dialog({
  title,
  children,
  onClose,
}: {
  title: string;
  children: ReactNode;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const previouslyFocused = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    ref.current?.focus();
    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
      previouslyFocused?.focus();
    };
  }, [onClose]);

  return (
    <div className="overlay" role="presentation" onClick={onClose}>
      <div
        className="dialog"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="dialog-title"
        tabIndex={-1}
        ref={ref}
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <h2 id="dialog-title">{title}</h2>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\priya\OneDrive\Desktop\COHORT\WEB DEV\PROJECT\UP-PET-Exam\src\components\MathText.tsx ===
import katex from "katex";
import { useMemo } from "react";

function renderChunk(text: string, display: boolean): string {
  try {
    return katex.renderToString(text, { displayMode: display, throwOnError: false });
  } catch {
    return text;
  }
}

export function MathText({ text }: { text: string }) {
  const html = useMemo(() => {
    const pattern = /\$\$([\s\S]+?)\$\$|\$([^$\n]+?)\$|\\\(([\s\S]+?)\\\)|\\\[([\s\S]+?)\\\]/g;
    let last = 0;
    let out = "";
    const source = text ?? "";
    for (const match of source.matchAll(pattern)) {
      const index = match.index ?? 0;
      out += escapeHtml(source.slice(last, index)).replaceAll("\n", "<br />");
      if (match[1] || match[4]) out += renderChunk(match[1] ?? match[4], true);
      else out += renderChunk(match[2] ?? match[3], false);
      last = index + match[0].length;
    }
    out += escapeHtml(source.slice(last)).replaceAll("\n", "<br />");
    return out;
  }, [text]);

  return <span className="math-text" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: html }} />;
}

function escapeHtml(value: string): string {
  return value
    .replaceAll("&", "&amp;")
    .replaceAll("<", "&lt;")
    .replaceAll(">", "&gt;");
}

export function pickText(
  value: { en?: string | null; hi?: string | null } | string | null | undefined,
  lang: "en" | "hi",
): string {
  if (!value) return "";
  if (typeof value === "string") return value;
  return (value[lang] || value.en || value.hi || "").toString();
}

import { getQuestionStatus, statusLabel } from "../engine/questionStatus";
import type { QuestionState, QuestionStatus } from "../types/exam";

const STATUSES: QuestionStatus[] = [
  "not-visited",
  "visited-unanswered",
  "answered",
  "marked",
  "answered-marked",
];

export function QuestionPalette({
  questionIds,
  states,
  currentIndex,
  onJump,
}: {
  questionIds: string[];
  states: Record<string, QuestionState>;
  currentIndex: number;
  onJump: (index: number) => void;
}) {
  const counts = Object.fromEntries(STATUSES.map((status) => [status, 0])) as Record<QuestionStatus, number>;
  for (const id of questionIds) counts[getQuestionStatus(states[id])] += 1;

  return (
    <div>
      <p className="muted">Question palette</p>
      <div className="palette" role="list">
        {questionIds.map((id, index) => {
          const status = getQuestionStatus(states[id]);
          return (
            <button
              key={id}
              type="button"
              className={`st-${status} ${index === currentIndex ? "current" : ""}`}
              aria-current={index === currentIndex ? "true" : undefined}
              aria-label={`Question ${index + 1}, ${statusLabel(status)}`}
              onClick={() => onJump(index)}
            >
              {index + 1}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
      <div className="legend">
        {STATUSES.map((status) => (
          <span key={status}>
            <i className={`swatch st-${status}`} aria-hidden="true" />
            {statusLabel(status)} ({counts[status]})
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import type {
  ExamRegistry,
  ExamValidation,
  LibraryExam,
  RegistryDefaults,
  RegistryExam,
  ResolvedExam,
  ValidationIssue,
} from "../types/exam";
import { fingerprintPaper } from "../engine/hash";
import { buildAnswerKey, stripAnswerKey } from "../engine/scoring";
import {
  parseExamPaper,
  resolveDuration,
  resolveMarkingScheme,
  validateExamPaper,
  validateRegistry,
} from "../engine/validateExam";
import { getImportedExam, listImportedExams } from "./importedLibrary";

export type LibrarySnapshot = {
  defaults: RegistryDefaults;
  bundled: LibraryExam[];
  imported: LibraryExam[];
  exams: LibraryExam[];
  registryIssues: ValidationIssue[];
};

const FALLBACK_DEFAULTS: RegistryDefaults = {
  durationMinutes: 120,
  durationNote: "Typical UP PET duration used because the paper file omitted it.",
  markingScheme: {
    marksPerCorrect: 1,
    marksPerIncorrect: -0.25,
    marksPerUnattempted: 0,
  },
  markingNote: "Standard PET practice marking used because the paper file omitted it.",
};

export async function loadRegistry(): Promise<{
  defaults: RegistryDefaults;
  exams: RegistryExam[];
  issues: ValidationIssue[];
}> {
  const response = await fetch("/exams/registry.json", { cache: "no-store" });
  if (!response.ok) {
    return {
      defaults: FALLBACK_DEFAULTS,
      exams: [],
      issues: [{ level: "error", code: "registry-missing", message: "Could not load /exams/registry.json." }],
    };
  }
  const raw: unknown = await response.json();
  const parsed = validateRegistry(raw);
  return {
    defaults: parsed.defaults ?? FALLBACK_DEFAULTS,
    exams: parsed.exams.filter((exam) => exam.status !== "draft"),
    issues: parsed.issues,
  };
}

async function peekPaperMeta(exam: RegistryExam): Promise<Pick<LibraryExam, "questionCount" | "durationMinutes" | "markingScheme" | "validationOk" | "validationIssues">> {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(exam.dataPath, { cache: "no-store" });
    if (!response.ok) {
      return {
        validationOk: false,
        validationIssues: [{ level: "error", code: "missing-file", message: `Exam file not found: ${exam.dataPath}` }],
      };
    }
    const raw: unknown = await response.json();
    const { paper, issues } = parseExamPaper(raw);
    if (!paper) {
      return { validationOk: false, validationIssues: issues };
    }
    const validation = validateExamPaper(paper);
    return {
      questionCount: paper.questions.length,
      durationMinutes: paper.durationMinutes ?? exam.durationMinutes,
      markingScheme: paper.markingScheme ?? exam.markingScheme,
      validationOk: validation.ok,
      validationIssues: [...issues, ...validation.issues],
    };
  } catch {
    return {
      validationOk: false,
      validationIssues: [{ level: "error", code: "parse-error", message: `Could not parse ${exam.dataPath}` }],
    };
  }
}

export async function loadLibrary(): Promise<LibrarySnapshot> {
  const registry = await loadRegistry();
  const bundled = await Promise.all(
    registry.exams.map(async (exam) => {
      const meta = await peekPaperMeta(exam);
      return { ...exam, ...meta, source: "bundled" as const };
    }),
  );
  const importedRecords = await listImportedExams();
  const imported: LibraryExam[] = importedRecords.map((record) => {
    const validation = validateExamPaper(record.paper);
    return {
      ...record.meta,
      questionCount: record.paper.questions.length,
      durationMinutes: record.paper.durationMinutes ?? record.meta.durationMinutes,
      markingScheme: record.paper.markingScheme ?? record.meta.markingScheme,
      validationOk: validation.ok,
      validationIssues: validation.issues,
      source: "imported",
    };
  });
  return {
    defaults: registry.defaults,
    bundled,
    imported,
    exams: [...bundled, ...imported],
    registryIssues: registry.issues,
  };
}

export async function loadResolvedExam(examId: string, defaults: RegistryDefaults): Promise<ResolvedExam> {
  const registry = await loadRegistry();
  const bundled = registry.exams.find((exam) => exam.id === examId);
  if (bundled) {
    const response = await fetch(bundled.dataPath, { cache: "no-store" });
    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Exam data file is missing: ${bundled.dataPath}`);
    }
    const raw: unknown = await response.json();
    return resolveLoadedPaper(bundled, raw, defaults, "bundled");
  }

  const imported = await getImportedExam(examId);
  if (!imported) {
    throw new Error(`Exam ${examId} was not found in the registry or local library.`);
  }
  return resolveLoadedPaper(imported.meta, imported.paper, defaults, "imported");
}

async function resolveLoadedPaper(
  registry: RegistryExam,
  raw: unknown,
  defaults: RegistryDefaults,
  source: "bundled" | "imported",
): Promise<ResolvedExam> {
  const parsed = parseExamPaper(raw);
  if (!parsed.paper) {
    throw new Error(parsed.issues[0]?.message ?? "Invalid exam file.");
  }
  const validation = validateExamPaper(parsed.paper);
  if (!validation.ok) {
    const firstError = validation.issues.find((issue) => issue.level === "error");
    throw new Error(firstError?.message ?? "Exam failed validation.");
  }
  const duration = resolveDuration(parsed.paper, registry, defaults);
  const marking = resolveMarkingScheme(parsed.paper, registry, defaults);
  return {
    registry,
    paper: parsed.paper,
    questions: stripAnswerKey(parsed.paper.questions),
    contexts: parsed.paper.contexts ?? [],
    durationMinutes: duration.minutes,
    durationSource: duration.source,
    markingScheme: marking.scheme,
    markingSource: marking.source,
    durationNote: duration.note,
    markingNote: marking.note,
    validation: { ...validation, issues: [...parsed.issues, ...validation.issues] },
    dataVersion: await fingerprintPaper(parsed.paper),
    answerKey: buildAnswerKey(parsed.paper.questions),
    source,
  };
}

export function examToRegistryExport(snapshot: LibrarySnapshot): ExamRegistry {
  return {
    schemaVersion: "1.0",
    defaults: snapshot.defaults,
    exams: snapshot.bundled.map(({ questionCount: _q, validationOk: _ok, validationIssues: _issues, source: _source, ...exam }) => exam),
  };
}

export function summarizeValidation(validation: ExamValidation | undefined): string {
  if (!validation) return "Not validated";
  const errors = validation.issues.filter((issue) => issue.level === "error").length;
  const warnings = validation.issues.filter((issue) => issue.level === "warning").length;
  if (errors) return `${errors} error${errors === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
  if (warnings) return `${warnings} warning${warnings === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
  return "Valid";
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\priya\OneDrive\Desktop\COHORT\WEB DEV\PROJECT\UP-PET-Exam\src\data\importedLibrary.ts ===
import type { ExamPaper, RegistryExam } from "../types/exam";

const DB_NAME = "up-pet-exam";
const STORE = "imported-exams";

type ImportedRecord = {
  id: string;
  meta: RegistryExam;
  paper: ExamPaper;
  importedAt: number;
};

function openDb(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, 1);
    request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
      const db = request.result;
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE)) {
        db.createObjectStore(STORE, { keyPath: "id" });
      }
    };
    request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
  });
}

export async function listImportedExams(): Promise<ImportedRecord[]> {
  const db = await openDb();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const tx = db.transaction(STORE, "readonly");
    const request = tx.objectStore(STORE).getAll();
    request.onsuccess = () => resolve((request.result as ImportedRecord[]) ?? []);
    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
  });
}

export async function saveImportedExam(record: ImportedRecord): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openDb();
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const tx = db.transaction(STORE, "readwrite");
    tx.objectStore(STORE).put(record);
    tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
    tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
  });
}

export async function getImportedExam(id: string): Promise<ImportedRecord | null> {
  const db = await openDb();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const tx = db.transaction(STORE, "readonly");
    const request = tx.objectStore(STORE).get(id);
    request.onsuccess = () => resolve((request.result as ImportedRecord | undefined) ?? null);
    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
  });
}

export async function deleteImportedExam(id: string): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openDb();
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const tx = db.transaction(STORE, "readwrite");
    tx.objectStore(STORE).delete(id);
    tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
    tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
  });
}

export async function clearImportedExams(): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openDb();
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const tx = db.transaction(STORE, "readwrite");
    tx.objectStore(STORE).clear();
    tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
    tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
  });
}

import type {
  ActiveAttempt,
  AttemptResult,
  ResultSummary,
  UserPreferences,
} from "../types/exam";

const PREFS_KEY = "up-pet:prefs";
const ACTIVE_KEY = "up-pet:active-attempt";
const HISTORY_KEY = "up-pet:history";
const RESULT_PREFIX = "up-pet:result:";

export const defaultPreferences: UserPreferences = {
  languageMode: "bilingual",
  theme: "dark",
  autoAdvance: false,
};

function readJson<T>(key: string, fallback: T): T {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
    if (!raw) return fallback;
    return JSON.parse(raw) as T;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

export function loadPreferences(): UserPreferences {
  return { ...defaultPreferences, ...readJson(PREFS_KEY, {}) };
}

export function savePreferences(prefs: UserPreferences): void {
  localStorage.setItem(PREFS_KEY, JSON.stringify(prefs));
}

export function loadActiveAttempt(): ActiveAttempt | null {
  const attempt = readJson<ActiveAttempt | null>(ACTIVE_KEY, null);
  if (!attempt || attempt.submitted) return null;
  return attempt;
}

export function saveActiveAttempt(attempt: ActiveAttempt): void {
  localStorage.setItem(ACTIVE_KEY, JSON.stringify(attempt));
}

export function clearActiveAttempt(): void {
  localStorage.removeItem(ACTIVE_KEY);
}

export function loadHistory(): ResultSummary[] {
  return readJson<ResultSummary[]>(HISTORY_KEY, []);
}

export function saveHistory(history: ResultSummary[]): void {
  localStorage.setItem(HISTORY_KEY, JSON.stringify(history));
}

export function appendResult(result: AttemptResult): void {
  localStorage.setItem(RESULT_PREFIX + result.attemptId, JSON.stringify(result));
  const history = loadHistory();
  const summary: ResultSummary = {
    attemptId: result.attemptId,
    examId: result.examId,
    examTitle: result.examTitle,
    submittedAt: result.submittedAt,
    score: result.breakdown.score,
    maxMarks: result.breakdown.maxMarks,
    percentage: result.breakdown.percentage,
    correct: result.breakdown.correct,
    incorrect: result.breakdown.incorrect,
    unanswered: result.breakdown.unanswered,
    autoSubmitted: result.autoSubmitted,
  };
  saveHistory([summary, ...history.filter((item) => item.attemptId !== result.attemptId)]);
}

export function loadResult(attemptId: string): AttemptResult | null {
  return readJson<AttemptResult | null>(RESULT_PREFIX + attemptId, null);
}

export function deleteHistoryForExam(examId: string): void {
  const history = loadHistory();
  const keep: ResultSummary[] = [];
  for (const item of history) {
    if (item.examId === examId) {
      localStorage.removeItem(RESULT_PREFIX + item.attemptId);
    } else {
      keep.push(item);
    }
  }
  saveHistory(keep);
}

export function clearAllLocalData(): void {
  const keys = Object.keys(localStorage).filter((key) => key.startsWith("up-pet:"));
  for (const key of keys) localStorage.removeItem(key);
}

export function examHistory(examId: string): ResultSummary[] {
  return loadHistory().filter((item) => item.examId === examId);
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { clearOption, createAttempt, selectOption, toggleMark } from "./attempt";
import { getQuestionStatus } from "./questionStatus";
import { remainingMs } from "./timer";
import { parseExamPaper, validateExamPaper } from "./validateExam";

describe("attempt state", () => {
  it("selects, replaces, and clears answers", () => {
    const attempt = createAttempt({
      examId: "e",
      dataVersion: "v",
      questionIds: ["Q1"],
      durationMinutes: 1,
      languageMode: "en",
      autoAdvance: false,
    });
    let state = selectOption(attempt.states.Q1, "B");
    expect(state.selectedOptionId).toBe("B");
    state = selectOption(state, "C");
    expect(state.selectedOptionId).toBe("C");
    state = clearOption(state);
    expect(state.selectedOptionId).toBeNull();
    expect(getQuestionStatus(state)).toBe("visited-unanswered");
  });

  it("tracks answered and marked states independently", () => {
    const attempt = createAttempt({
      examId: "e",
      dataVersion: "v",
      questionIds: ["Q1"],
      durationMinutes: 1,
      languageMode: "en",
      autoAdvance: false,
    });
    let state = toggleMark(attempt.states.Q1);
    expect(getQuestionStatus(state)).toBe("marked");
    state = selectOption(state, "A");
    expect(getQuestionStatus(state)).toBe("answered-marked");
  });

  it("uses an absolute deadline rather than a decrementing counter", () => {
    const attempt = createAttempt({
      examId: "e",
      dataVersion: "v",
      questionIds: ["Q1"],
      durationMinutes: 2,
      languageMode: "en",
      autoAdvance: false,
      now: 10_000,
    });
    expect(attempt.deadlineAt).toBe(10_000 + 2 * 60 * 1000);
    expect(remainingMs(attempt.deadlineAt, 10_000 + 30_000)).toBe(90_000);
  });
});

describe("validation", () => {
  it("rejects duplicate question IDs and invalid answer labels", () => {
    const { paper } = parseExamPaper({

=== FILE: C:\Users\priya\OneDrive\Desktop\COHORT\WEB DEV\PROJECT\UP-PET-Exam\src\engine\attempt.ts ===
import type { ActiveAttempt, LanguageMode, QuestionState } from "../types/exam";
import { createQuestionState } from "./questionStatus";
import { createDeadline } from "./timer";

export function createAttempt(input: {
  examId: string;
  dataVersion: string;
  questionIds: string[];
  durationMinutes: number;
  languageMode: LanguageMode;
  autoAdvance: boolean;
  now?: number;
}): ActiveAttempt {
  const startedAt = input.now ?? Date.now();
  const states: Record<string, QuestionState> = {};
  for (const id of input.questionIds) {
    states[id] = createQuestionState();
  }
  if (input.questionIds[0]) {
    states[input.questionIds[0]] = { ...states[input.questionIds[0]], visited: true };
  }
  return {
    attemptId: crypto.randomUUID(),
    examId: input.examId,
    dataVersion: input.dataVersion,
    startedAt,
    deadlineAt: createDeadline(input.durationMinutes, startedAt),
    currentIndex: 0,
    languageMode: input.languageMode,
    autoAdvance: input.autoAdvance,
    states,
    submitted: false,
  };
}

export function selectOption(state: QuestionState, optionId: string): QuestionState {
  return { ...state, selectedOptionId: optionId, visited: true };
}

export function clearOption(state: QuestionState): QuestionState {
  return { ...state, selectedOptionId: null, visited: true };
}

export function toggleMark(state: QuestionState): QuestionState {
  return { ...state, marked: !state.marked, visited: true };
}

export function visitQuestion(state: QuestionState | undefined): QuestionState {
  return { ...(state ?? createQuestionState()), visited: true };
}

export function addTimeSpent(state: QuestionState, deltaMs: number): QuestionState {
  if (deltaMs <= 0) return state;
  return { ...state, timeSpentMs: state.timeSpentMs + deltaMs };
}

export function isAttemptExpired(attempt: ActiveAttempt, now = Date.now()): boolean {
  return now >= attempt.deadlineAt;
}

export function attemptCounts(attempt: ActiveAttempt, questionIds: string[]) {
  let answered = 0;
  let marked = 0;
  let notVisited = 0;
  for (const id of questionIds) {
    const state = attempt.states[id];
    if (!state?.visited) notVisited += 1;
    if (state?.selectedOptionId) answered += 1;
    if (state?.marked) marked += 1;
  }
  return {
    answered,
    unanswered: questionIds.length - answered,
    marked,
    notVisited,
    total: questionIds.length,
  };
}

import { applyOptionClick } from "./optionClick";

describe("option click rules", () => {
  it("selects on single click and clears on double click of the same option", () => {
    let selected: string | null = null;
    const first = applyOptionClick({ selectedOptionId: selected, optionId: "A", now: 1000, lastClick: null });
    selected = first.selectedOptionId;
    expect(selected).toBe("A");

      selectedOptionId: selected,
      optionId: "A",
      now: 1200,
      lastClick: first.lastClick,
    });
    expect(second.selectedOptionId).toBeNull();
  });

  it("replaces the selection when a different option is clicked", () => {
    const first = applyOptionClick({ selectedOptionId: null, optionId: "A", now: 1000, lastClick: null });
    const second = applyOptionClick({
      selectedOptionId: first.selectedOptionId,
      optionId: "B",
      now: 1100,
      lastClick: first.lastClick,
    });
    expect(second.selectedOptionId).toBe("B");
  });

=== FILE: C:\Users\priya\OneDrive\Desktop\COHORT\WEB DEV\PROJECT\UP-PET-Exam\src\engine\hash.ts ===
export async function sha256Hex(value: string): Promise<string> {
  const encoded = new TextEncoder().encode(value);
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", encoded);
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest))
    .map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join("");
}

export function fingerprintPaper(paper: unknown): Promise<string> {
  return sha256Hex(JSON.stringify(paper));
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\priya\OneDrive\Desktop\COHORT\WEB DEV\PROJECT\UP-PET-Exam\src\engine\optionClick.ts ===
export type ClickMemory = { optionId: string; at: number };

const DOUBLE_CLICK_MS = 350;

export function applyOptionClick(input: {
  selectedOptionId: string | null;
  optionId: string;
  now: number;
  lastClick: ClickMemory | null;
}): { selectedOptionId: string | null; lastClick: ClickMemory } {
  const isDouble =
    input.selectedOptionId === input.optionId &&
    input.lastClick?.optionId === input.optionId &&
    input.now - (input.lastClick?.at ?? 0) <= DOUBLE_CLICK_MS;

  if (isDouble) {
    return { selectedOptionId: null, lastClick: { optionId: input.optionId, at: 0 } };
  }

  return {
    selectedOptionId: input.optionId,
    lastClick: { optionId: input.optionId, at: input.now },
  };
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\priya\OneDrive\Desktop\COHORT\WEB DEV\PROJECT\UP-PET-Exam\src\engine\questionStatus.ts ===
import type { QuestionState, QuestionStatus } from "../types/exam";

export function createQuestionState(): QuestionState {
  return {
    visited: false,
    marked: false,
    selectedOptionId: null,
    timeSpentMs: 0,
  };
}

export function getQuestionStatus(state: QuestionState | undefined): QuestionStatus {
  if (!state || !state.visited) return "not-visited";
  const answered = Boolean(state.selectedOptionId);
  if (state.marked && answered) return "answered-marked";
  if (state.marked) return "marked";
  if (answered) return "answered";
  return "visited-unanswered";
}

export function isAnswered(state: QuestionState | undefined): boolean {
  return Boolean(state?.selectedOptionId);
}

export function statusLabel(status: QuestionStatus): string {
  switch (status) {
    case "not-visited":
      return "Not visited";
    case "visited-unanswered":
      return "Visited, unanswered";
    case "answered":
      return "Answered";
    case "marked":
      return "Marked for review";
    case "answered-marked":
      return "Answered and marked";
  }
}

const scheme: MarkingScheme = {
  marksPerCorrect: 1,
  marksPerIncorrect: -0.25,
  marksPerUnattempted: 0,
};

function q(id: string, answer: string | null, extra?: Partial<ExamQuestion>): ExamQuestion {
  return {
    id,
    question: { en: id, hi: id },
    options: [
      { id: "A", en: "A", hi: "A" },
      { id: "B", en: "B", hi: "B" },
      { id: "C", en: "C", hi: "C" },
      { id: "D", en: "D", hi: "D" },
    ],
    correctAnswer: answer,
    verificationStatus: answer ? "verified" : "unresolved",
    subject: extra?.subject ?? "General",
    ...extra,
  };
}

    expect(result.breakdown.incorrect).toBe(1);

=== FILE: C:\Users\priya\OneDrive\Desktop\COHORT\WEB DEV\PROJECT\UP-PET-Exam\src\engine\scoring.ts ===
import type {
  ActiveAttempt,
  AnswerKeyEntry,
  AttemptResult,
  ExamQuestion,
  MarkingScheme,
  QuestionOutcome,
  QuestionResult,
  ScoreBreakdown,
} from "../types/exam";
import { isEvaluableQuestion } from "./validateExam";

export const SCORING_POLICY =
  "Questions with a verified answer key are scored using the exam marking scheme. Marked-for-review answers are scored if an option is selected. Questions with unresolved, unreadable, or missing keys are excluded from correct/incorrect counts and contribute 0 marks. They still appear in unanswered/attempted statistics. Maximum marks equal evaluable questions × marks per correct answer.";

export function buildAnswerKey(questions: ExamQuestion[]): Record<string, AnswerKeyEntry> {
  const key: Record<string, AnswerKeyEntry> = {};
  for (const question of questions) {
    key[question.id] = {
      questionId: question.id,
      correctAnswer: question.correctAnswer ?? null,
      correctAnswerText: question.correctAnswerText,
      explanation: question.explanation ?? null,
      verificationStatus: (question.verificationStatus ?? "verified").toString(),
      issues: question.issues ?? [],
      evaluable: isEvaluableQuestion(question),
    };
  }
  return key;
}

export function stripAnswerKey(questions: ExamQuestion[]): Array<Omit<ExamQuestion, "correctAnswer" | "correctAnswerText" | "explanation" | "answerSource" | "issues">> {
  return questions.map((question) => ({
    id: question.id,
    originalQuestionNumber: question.originalQuestionNumber,
    subject: question.subject,
    contextId: question.contextId,
    question: question.question,
    options: question.options,
    sourcePage: question.sourcePage,
    verificationStatus: question.verificationStatus,
    optionsIncomplete: question.optionsIncomplete,
  }));
}

function roundScore(value: number): number {
  return Math.round(value * 100) / 100;
}

export function scoreAttempt(input: {
  attempt: ActiveAttempt;
  examTitle: string;
  questions: Array<{ id: string; subject?: string | null }>;
  answerKey: Record<string, AnswerKeyEntry>;
  markingScheme: MarkingScheme;
  markingSource: string;
  autoSubmitted: boolean;
  now?: number;
}): AttemptResult {
  const submittedAt = input.now ?? Date.now();
  const questionResults: QuestionResult[] = [];

  for (const question of input.questions) {
    const state = input.attempt.states[question.id];
    const key = input.answerKey[question.id];
    const selected = state?.selectedOptionId ?? null;
    const evaluable = Boolean(key?.evaluable && key.correctAnswer);
    let outcome: QuestionOutcome = "unanswered";
    if (!selected) {
      outcome = evaluable ? "unanswered" : "unevaluated";
      if (!evaluable && !selected) outcome = "unevaluated";
    } else if (!evaluable) {
      outcome = "unevaluated";
    } else if (selected === key.correctAnswer) {
      outcome = "correct";
    } else {
      outcome = "incorrect";
    }
    if (!selected && evaluable) outcome = "unanswered";
    if (!selected && !evaluable) outcome = "unevaluated";

    questionResults.push({
      questionId: question.id,
      subject: question.subject?.trim() || "Unspecified",
      selectedOptionId: selected,
      marked: Boolean(state?.marked),
      timeSpentMs: state?.timeSpentMs ?? 0,
      outcome,
      correctAnswer: key?.correctAnswer ?? null,
      evaluable,
    });
  }

  const correct = questionResults.filter((item) => item.outcome === "correct").length;
  const incorrect = questionResults.filter((item) => item.outcome === "incorrect").length;
  const unansweredEvaluable = questionResults.filter((item) => item.outcome === "unanswered").length;
  const unevaluated = questionResults.filter((item) => item.outcome === "unevaluated").length;
  const markedForReview = questionResults.filter((item) => item.marked).length;
  const correctMarkedForReview = questionResults.filter((item) => item.marked && item.outcome === "correct").length;
  const attempted = questionResults.filter((item) => item.selectedOptionId).length;
  const evaluableCount = questionResults.filter((item) => item.evaluable).length;
  const { marksPerCorrect, marksPerIncorrect, marksPerUnattempted } = input.markingScheme;

  const score = roundScore(
    correct * marksPerCorrect +
      incorrect * marksPerIncorrect +
      unansweredEvaluable * marksPerUnattempted,
  );
  const maxMarks = roundScore(evaluableCount * marksPerCorrect);
  const judged = correct + incorrect;
  const accuracy = judged === 0 ? null : roundScore((correct / judged) * 100);
  const percentage = maxMarks === 0 ? null : roundScore((score / maxMarks) * 100);

  const breakdown: ScoreBreakdown = {
    correct,
    incorrect,
    unanswered: unansweredEvaluable + questionResults.filter((item) => !item.selectedOptionId && item.evaluable === false).length,
    unevaluated,
    markedForReview,
    correctMarkedForReview,
    attempted,
    accuracy,
    score,
    maxMarks,
    percentage,
    evaluableCount,
  };

  // Keep unanswered count as all questions without a selected option.
  breakdown.unanswered = questionResults.filter((item) => !item.selectedOptionId).length;

  const subjectMap = new Map<string, QuestionResult[]>();
  for (const result of questionResults) {
    const list = subjectMap.get(result.subject) ?? [];
    list.push(result);
    subjectMap.set(result.subject, list);
  }

  const subjectStats = [...subjectMap.entries()].map(([subject, rows]) => {
    const sCorrect = rows.filter((row) => row.outcome === "correct").length;
    const sIncorrect = rows.filter((row) => row.outcome === "incorrect").length;
    const sUnanswered = rows.filter((row) => !row.selectedOptionId).length;
    const sUnevaluated = rows.filter((row) => row.outcome === "unevaluated").length;
    const sAttempted = rows.filter((row) => row.selectedOptionId).length;
    const sJudged = sCorrect + sIncorrect;
    return {
      subject,
      correct: sCorrect,
      incorrect: sIncorrect,
      unanswered: sUnanswered,
      unevaluated: sUnevaluated,
      attempted: sAttempted,
      accuracy: sJudged === 0 ? null : roundScore((sCorrect / sJudged) * 100),
    };
  });

  const timeUsedMs = Math.max(0, submittedAt - input.attempt.startedAt);
  const timeRemainingMs = Math.max(0, input.attempt.deadlineAt - submittedAt);

  return {
    attemptId: input.attempt.attemptId,
    examId: input.attempt.examId,
    examTitle: input.examTitle,
    dataVersion: input.attempt.dataVersion,
    startedAt: input.attempt.startedAt,
    submittedAt,
    timeUsedMs,
    timeRemainingMs,
    autoSubmitted: input.autoSubmitted,
    languageMode: input.attempt.languageMode,
    markingScheme: input.markingScheme,
    markingSource: input.markingSource,
    scoringPolicy: SCORING_POLICY,
    breakdown,
    questionResults,
    subjectStats,
  };
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\priya\OneDrive\Desktop\COHORT\WEB DEV\PROJECT\UP-PET-Exam\src\engine\timer.ts ===
export function formatDuration(totalSeconds: number): string {
  const safe = Math.max(0, Math.floor(totalSeconds));
  const hours = Math.floor(safe / 3600);
  const minutes = Math.floor((safe % 3600) / 60);
  const seconds = safe % 60;
  const mm = String(minutes).padStart(2, "0");
  const ss = String(seconds).padStart(2, "0");
  if (hours > 0) return `${String(hours).padStart(2, "0")}:${mm}:${ss}`;
  return `${mm}:${ss}`;
}

export function remainingMs(deadlineAt: number, now = Date.now()): number {
  return Math.max(0, deadlineAt - now);
}

export function createDeadline(durationMinutes: number, startedAt = Date.now()): number {
  return startedAt + durationMinutes * 60 * 1000;
}

export function timerUrgency(remaining: number): "normal" | "warn" | "final" {
  if (remaining <= 60_000) return "final";
  if (remaining <= 5 * 60_000) return "warn";
  return "normal";
}

import type {
  ExamPaper,
  ExamQuestion,
  ExamValidation,
  MarkingScheme,
  RegistryDefaults,
  RegistryExam,
  ValidationIssue,
} from "../types/exam";

const OPTION_ID = /^[A-Za-z0-9]+$/;

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return Boolean(value) && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function asString(value: unknown): string | null {
  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim() ? value.trim() : null;
}

function bilingualPresent(value: unknown): { en: boolean; hi: boolean } {
  if (!isRecord(value)) return { en: false, hi: false };
  return {
    en: typeof value.en === "string" && value.en.trim().length > 0,
    hi: typeof value.hi === "string" && value.hi.trim().length > 0,
  };
}

export function isEvaluableQuestion(question: ExamQuestion): boolean {
  const status = (question.verificationStatus ?? "verified").toString().toLowerCase();
  if (["unresolved", "unreadable", "disputed", "incomplete"].includes(status)) {
    return false;
  }
  if (question.optionsIncomplete) return false;
  if (!question.correctAnswer) return false;
  return question.options.some((option) => option.id === question.correctAnswer);
}

export function validateRegistry(raw: unknown): {
  ok: boolean;
  issues: ValidationIssue[];
  defaults: RegistryDefaults | null;
  exams: RegistryExam[];
} {
  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
  if (!isRecord(raw)) {
    return { ok: false, issues: [{ level: "error", code: "registry-invalid", message: "Registry is not an object." }], defaults: null, exams: [] };
  }

  const defaultsRaw = isRecord(raw.defaults) ? raw.defaults : {};
  const marking = isRecord(defaultsRaw.markingScheme) ? defaultsRaw.markingScheme : {};
  const defaults: RegistryDefaults = {
    durationMinutes: Number(defaultsRaw.durationMinutes) || 120,
    durationNote: asString(defaultsRaw.durationNote) ?? "",
    markingNote: asString(defaultsRaw.markingNote) ?? "",
    markingScheme: {
      marksPerCorrect: Number(marking.marksPerCorrect ?? 1),
      marksPerIncorrect: Number(marking.marksPerIncorrect ?? 0),
      marksPerUnattempted: Number(marking.marksPerUnattempted ?? 0),
    },
  };

  if (!Array.isArray(raw.exams)) {
    issues.push({ level: "error", code: "registry-exams", message: "Registry exams must be an array." });
    return { ok: false, issues, defaults, exams: [] };
  }

  const exams: RegistryExam[] = [];
  const ids = new Set<string>();
  for (const entry of raw.exams) {
    if (!isRecord(entry)) {
      issues.push({ level: "error", code: "registry-entry", message: "A registry entry is not an object." });
      continue;
    }
    const id = asString(entry.id);
    const title = asString(entry.title);
    const dataPath = asString(entry.dataPath);
    if (!id || !title || !dataPath) {
      issues.push({
        level: "error",
        code: "registry-entry-missing",
        message: `Registry entry is missing id, title, or dataPath (${id ?? "unknown"}).`,
      });
      continue;
    }
    if (ids.has(id)) {
      issues.push({ level: "error", code: "registry-duplicate-id", message: `Duplicate exam ID in registry: ${id}` });
      continue;
    }
    ids.add(id);
    exams.push({
      id,
      title,
      year: typeof entry.year === "number" ? entry.year : null,
      examDate: asString(entry.examDate),
      shift: asString(entry.shift),
      paperSet: asString(entry.paperSet),
      category: asString(entry.category) ?? "previous-year",
      dataPath,
      status: asString(entry.status) ?? "published",
      durationMinutes: typeof entry.durationMinutes === "number" ? entry.durationMinutes : null,
      markingScheme: isRecord(entry.markingScheme) ? (entry.markingScheme as Partial<MarkingScheme>) : null,
      source: "bundled",
    });
  }

  return { ok: issues.every((issue) => issue.level !== "error"), issues, defaults, exams };
}

export function parseExamPaper(raw: unknown): { paper: ExamPaper | null; issues: ValidationIssue[] } {
  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
  if (!isRecord(raw)) {
    return { paper: null, issues: [{ level: "error", code: "invalid-json", message: "Exam file is not a JSON object." }] };
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(raw.questions) || raw.questions.length === 0) {
    issues.push({ level: "error", code: "no-questions", message: "Exam file has no questions." });
    return { paper: null, issues };
  }

  const questions: ExamQuestion[] = [];
  for (const item of raw.questions) {
    if (!isRecord(item)) {
      issues.push({ level: "error", code: "question-invalid", message: "A question entry is not an object." });
      continue;
    }
    const id = asString(item.id);
    if (!id) {
      issues.push({ level: "error", code: "question-id", message: "A question is missing an id." });
      continue;
    }
    const optionsRaw = Array.isArray(item.options) ? item.options : [];
    const options = optionsRaw.flatMap((option) => {
      if (!isRecord(option)) return [];
      const optionId = asString(option.id);
      if (!optionId) return [];
      return [{ id: optionId, en: asString(option.en), hi: asString(option.hi) }];
    });
    questions.push({
      id,
      originalQuestionNumber: asString(item.originalQuestionNumber) ?? questions.length + 1,
      subject: asString(item.subject),
      contextId: asString(item.contextId),
      question: isRecord(item.question)
        ? { en: asString(item.question.en), hi: asString(item.question.hi) }
        : { en: null, hi: null },
      options,
      correctAnswer: asString(item.correctAnswer),
      correctAnswerText: isRecord(item.correctAnswerText)
        ? { en: asString(item.correctAnswerText.en), hi: asString(item.correctAnswerText.hi) }
        : undefined,
      answerSource: asString(item.answerSource),
      explanation: asString(item.explanation),
      sourcePage: typeof item.sourcePage === "number" ? item.sourcePage : null,
      verificationStatus: asString(item.verificationStatus) ?? "verified",
      issues: Array.isArray(item.issues) ? item.issues.filter((value): value is string => typeof value === "string") : [],
      optionsIncomplete: Boolean(item.optionsIncomplete),
    });
  }

  const paper: ExamPaper = {
    schemaVersion: asString(raw.schemaVersion) ?? "1.0",
    examId: asString(raw.examId) ?? "unknown-exam",
    title: asString(raw.title) ?? "Untitled exam",
    titleHi: asString(raw.titleHi) ?? undefined,
    examDate: asString(raw.examDate),
    shift: asString(raw.shift),
    paperSet: asString(raw.paperSet),
    durationMinutes: typeof raw.durationMinutes === "number" ? raw.durationMinutes : null,
    totalQuestions: typeof raw.totalQuestions === "number" ? raw.totalQuestions : questions.length,
    markingScheme: isRecord(raw.markingScheme) ? (raw.markingScheme as Partial<MarkingScheme>) : null,
    metadata: isRecord(raw.metadata) ? raw.metadata : {},
    conductingBody: isRecord(raw.conductingBody)
      ? { en: asString(raw.conductingBody.en), hi: asString(raw.conductingBody.hi) }
      : undefined,
    contexts: Array.isArray(raw.contexts) ? (raw.contexts as ExamPaper["contexts"]) : [],
    questions,
  };

  return { paper, issues };
}

export function validateExamPaper(paper: ExamPaper): ExamValidation {
  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const optionCounts: Record<string, number> = {};
  const unresolvedQuestionIds: string[] = [];
  let missingTranslationCount = 0;

  if (!paper.examId) {
    issues.push({ level: "error", code: "exam-id", message: "Exam is missing examId." });
  }
  if (!paper.title) {
    issues.push({ level: "error", code: "exam-title", message: "Exam is missing a title." });
  }
  if (paper.questions.length === 0) {
    issues.push({ level: "error", code: "no-questions", message: "Exam has no usable questions." });
  }

  for (const question of paper.questions) {
    if (seen.has(question.id)) {
      issues.push({
        level: "error",
        code: "duplicate-id",
        message: `Duplicate question ID ${question.id}.`,
        questionId: question.id,
      });
    }
    seen.add(question.id);

    const count = question.options.length;
    optionCounts[String(count)] = (optionCounts[String(count)] ?? 0) + 1;
    if (count < 2) {
      issues.push({
        level: question.optionsIncomplete ? "warning" : "error",
        code: "missing-options",
        message: `Question ${question.id} has fewer than two options.`,
        questionId: question.id,
      });
    }

    const optionIds = new Set<string>();
    for (const option of question.options) {
      if (!OPTION_ID.test(option.id)) {
        issues.push({
          level: "error",
          code: "invalid-option-id",
          message: `Question ${question.id} has an invalid option label ${option.id}.`,
          questionId: question.id,
        });
      }
      if (optionIds.has(option.id)) {
        issues.push({
          level: "error",
          code: "duplicate-option",
          message: `Question ${question.id} repeats option ${option.id}.`,
          questionId: question.id,
        });
      }
      optionIds.add(option.id);
      const optionLang = bilingualPresent(option);
      if (!optionLang.en && !optionLang.hi) {
        issues.push({
          level: "error",
          code: "empty-option",
          message: `Question ${question.id} option ${option.id} has no text.`,
          questionId: question.id,
        });
      }
    }

    const qLang = bilingualPresent(question.question);
    if (!qLang.en && !qLang.hi) {
      issues.push({
        level: "error",
        code: "empty-question",
        message: `Question ${question.id} has no English or Hindi text.`,
        questionId: question.id,
      });
    } else if (!qLang.en || !qLang.hi) {
      missingTranslationCount += 1;
      issues.push({
        level: "warning",
        code: "missing-translation",
        message: `Question ${question.id} is missing a language variant.`,
        questionId: question.id,
      });
    }

    if (question.correctAnswer && !optionIds.has(question.correctAnswer)) {
      issues.push({
        level: "error",
        code: "invalid-answer-label",
        message: `Question ${question.id} answer key ${question.correctAnswer} does not match any option.`,
        questionId: question.id,
      });
    }

    if (!isEvaluableQuestion(question)) {
      unresolvedQuestionIds.push(question.id);
      issues.push({
        level: "warning",
        code: "unresolved-key",
        message: `Question ${question.id} has no verified scorable answer key.`,
        questionId: question.id,
      });
    }
  }

  if (paper.totalQuestions && paper.totalQuestions !== paper.questions.length) {
    issues.push({
      level: "warning",
      code: "count-mismatch",
      message: `Declared totalQuestions is ${paper.totalQuestions}, but ${paper.questions.length} questions were found.`,
    });
  }

  const errorCount = issues.filter((issue) => issue.level === "error").length;
  return {
    ok: errorCount === 0,
    examId: paper.examId,
    title: paper.title,
    questionCount: paper.questions.length,
    optionCounts,
    unresolvedQuestionIds,
    missingTranslationCount,
    issues,
  };
}

export function resolveMarkingScheme(
  paper: ExamPaper,
  registry: RegistryExam,
  defaults: RegistryDefaults,
): { scheme: MarkingScheme; source: "paper" | "registry" | "defaults"; note?: string } {
  const paperScheme = paper.markingScheme;
  if (
    paperScheme &&
    typeof paperScheme.marksPerCorrect === "number" &&
    typeof paperScheme.marksPerIncorrect === "number" &&
    typeof paperScheme.marksPerUnattempted === "number"
  ) {
    return {
      scheme: {
        marksPerCorrect: paperScheme.marksPerCorrect,
        marksPerIncorrect: paperScheme.marksPerIncorrect,
        marksPerUnattempted: paperScheme.marksPerUnattempted,
      },
      source: "paper",
    };
  }
  if (
    registry.markingScheme &&
    typeof registry.markingScheme.marksPerCorrect === "number" &&
    typeof registry.markingScheme.marksPerIncorrect === "number" &&
    typeof registry.markingScheme.marksPerUnattempted === "number"
  ) {
    return {
      scheme: {
        marksPerCorrect: registry.markingScheme.marksPerCorrect,
        marksPerIncorrect: registry.markingScheme.marksPerIncorrect,
        marksPerUnattempted: registry.markingScheme.marksPerUnattempted,
      },
      source: "registry",
    };
  }
  return {
    scheme: defaults.markingScheme,
    source: "defaults",
    note: defaults.markingNote,
  };
}

export function resolveDuration(
  paper: ExamPaper,
  registry: RegistryExam,
  defaults: RegistryDefaults,
): { minutes: number; source: "paper" | "registry" | "defaults"; note?: string } {
  if (typeof paper.durationMinutes === "number" && paper.durationMinutes > 0) {
    return { minutes: paper.durationMinutes, source: "paper" };
  }
  if (typeof registry.durationMinutes === "number" && registry.durationMinutes > 0) {
    return { minutes: registry.durationMinutes, source: "registry" };
  }
  return { minutes: defaults.durationMinutes, source: "defaults", note: defaults.durationNote };
}

export function sanitizeQuestions(questions: ExamQuestion[]): ExamQuestion["id"][] {
  return questions.map((question) => question.id);
}

export function ExamPage() {

  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [exam, setExam] = useState<ResolvedExam | null>(null);
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState<ActiveAttempt | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [remaining, setRemaining] = useState(0);
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState(false);
  const [paletteOpen, setPaletteOpen] = useState(false);
  const lastClick = useRef<ClickMemory | null>(null);
  const lastTick = useRef(Date.now());
  const submitting = useRef(false);
  const attemptRef = useRef<ActiveAttempt | null>(null);

    let cancelled = false;

      try {
        const library = await loadLibrary();
        const resolved = await loadResolvedExam(examId, library.defaults);
        if (cancelled) return;
        const stored = loadActiveAttempt();
        if (!stored || stored.examId !== examId) {
          setError("No active attempt. Open the instructions screen to start the timer.");
          return;

        setAttempt(stored);

    if (submitting.current || current.submitted) return;
    submitting.current = true;
    const frozen: ActiveAttempt = { ...current, submitted: true, submittedAt: Date.now() };
    const result = scoreAttempt({
      attempt: frozen,
      examTitle: resolved.registry.title,
      questions: resolved.questions,
      answerKey: resolved.answerKey,
      markingScheme: resolved.markingScheme,
      markingSource: resolved.markingSource,
      autoSubmitted,
    });
    appendResult(result);
    clearActiveAttempt();
    navigate(`/results/${result.attemptId}`, { replace: true });
  }, [navigate]);

    if (!attempt || !exam) return;
    const tick = () => {
      const now = Date.now();
      const current = attemptRef.current;
      if (!current || current.submitted) return;
      const question = exam.questions[current.currentIndex];
      if (question) {
        const state = addTimeSpent(current.states[question.id], now - lastTick.current);
        const next = { ...current, states: { ...current.states, [question.id]: state } };
        attemptRef.current = next;
        saveActiveAttempt(next);
      }
      lastTick.current = now;
      const left = remainingMs(current.deadlineAt, now);
      setRemaining(left);
      if (left <= 0) finalize(exam, attemptRef.current ?? current, true);
    };
    tick();
    const id = window.setInterval(tick, 250);
    const onVis = () => { lastTick.current = Date.now(); tick(); };
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
    window.addEventListener("focus", onVis);
    return () => {
      window.clearInterval(id);
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
      window.removeEventListener("focus", onVis);
    };

  const counts = attempt ? attemptCounts(attempt, questionIds) : null;

      ...attempt,
      currentIndex: index,
      states: { ...attempt.states, [id]: visitQuestion(attempt.states[id]) },
    });
    setPaletteOpen(false);
  };

    const nextState = mutator(attempt.states[question.id]);
    persist({ ...attempt, states: { ...attempt.states, [question.id]: nextState } });
    return nextState;
  };

  const onOption = (optionId: string) => {

      selectedOptionId: attempt.states[question.id]?.selectedOptionId ?? null,
      optionId,
      now: Date.now(),
      lastClick: lastClick.current,
    });
    lastClick.current = result.lastClick;
    const nextState = result.selectedOptionId
      ? selectOption(attempt.states[question.id], result.selectedOptionId)
      : clearOption(attempt.states[question.id]);
    const nextAttempt = { ...attempt, states: { ...attempt.states, [question.id]: nextState } };
    persist(nextAttempt);
    if (result.selectedOptionId && attempt.autoAdvance && attempt.currentIndex < exam.questions.length - 1) {
      goTo(attempt.currentIndex + 1);
    }
  };

  const isLast = attempt.currentIndex === exam.questions.length - 1;
  const mode = attempt.languageMode;
  const langs: Array<"en" | "hi"> = mode === "bilingual" ? ["hi", "en"] : [mode];

  return (
    <div className="exam-shell">
      <header className="exam-top">
        <div>
          <h1>{exam.registry.title}</h1>
          <div className="muted">Question {attempt.currentIndex + 1} of {exam.questions.length}{question.subject ? ` · ${question.subject}` : ""}</div>
        </div>
        <div className={`timer ${urgency}`} aria-live="polite" aria-label="Time remaining">
          {formatDuration(remaining / 1000)}
        </div>
      </header>
      <div className="exam-body">
        <main id="main" className="question-pane">
          <div className="toolbar">
            <label>
              Language{" "}
              <select
                value={mode}
                onChange={(event) => persist({ ...attempt, languageMode: event.target.value as LanguageMode })}
                aria-label="Question language"

            );

          {question.options.map((option) => {
            const selected = attempt.states[question.id]?.selectedOptionId === option.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={option.id}
                type="button"
                className={`option ${selected ? "selected" : ""}`}
                aria-pressed={selected}
                onClick={() => onOption(option.id)}

                <span className="oid">{option.id}</span>

import { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

export function HistoryPage() {
  const [history, setHistory] = useState(loadHistory());
  const [clearOpen, setClearOpen] = useState(false);

  return (
    <main id="main" className="page">
      <h1>Attempt history</h1>
      <p className="muted">Results stay in this browser. They are not synced and can be lost if site data is cleared.</p>
      <div className="review-grid">
        {history.map((item) => (
          <article className="card" key={item.attemptId}>
            <h2>{item.examTitle}</h2>
            <p>{new Date(item.submittedAt).toLocaleString()} · {item.score}/{item.maxMarks}{item.percentage != null ? ` (${item.percentage}%)` : ""} · {item.autoSubmitted ? "Auto-submitted" : "Submitted"}</p>
            <div className="actions">
              <Link className="primary" to={`/results/${item.attemptId}`}>Open result</Link>
              <button type="button" className="ghost" onClick={() => {
                if (confirm(`Delete all stored results for ${item.examTitle}?`)) {
                  deleteHistoryForExam(item.examId);
                  setHistory(loadHistory());
                }
              }}>Delete this exam's history</button>
            </div>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>
      {history.length === 0 ? <p className="notice">No completed attempts yet.</p> : null}
      <div className="actions">
        <button type="button" className="danger" onClick={() => setClearOpen(true)}>Clear all local data</button>
      </div>
      {clearOpen ? (
        <Dialog title="Clear local data?" onClose={() => setClearOpen(false)}>
          <p>This removes in-progress attempts, results, preferences, and browser-imported exams from this device. Bundled exam files are not deleted.</p>
          <div className="actions">
            <button type="button" className="ghost" onClick={() => setClearOpen(false)}>Cancel</button>
            <button type="button" className="danger" onClick={async () => {
              clearAllLocalData();
              await clearImportedExams();
              setHistory([]);
              setClearOpen(false);
            }}>Clear everything</button>
          </div>
        </Dialog>
      ) : null}

  );

import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { loadLibrary, loadResolvedExam } from "../data/examLibrary";

  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [exam, setExam] = useState<ResolvedExam | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

      } catch (err) {
        if (!cancelled) setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Could not load this exam.");
      }
    })();
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [examId]);

  if (!exam) return <main className="page"><p>Loading exam instructions…</p></main>;

      durationMinutes: exam.durationMinutes,
      languageMode: prefs.languageMode,
      autoAdvance: prefs.autoAdvance,
    });
    saveActiveAttempt(attempt);
    navigate(`/exam/${encodeURIComponent(examId)}`);

    <main id="main" className="page">
      <p><Link to="/">← Library</Link></p>
      <h1>{exam.registry.title}</h1>
      <p className="muted">{exam.paper.titleHi || exam.paper.title}</p>
      <section className="card">
        <p><strong>Questions:</strong> {exam.questions.length}</p>
        <p><strong>Duration:</strong> {exam.durationMinutes} minutes ({exam.durationSource}{exam.durationNote ? ` — ${exam.durationNote}` : ""})</p>
        <p>
          <strong>Maximum marks:</strong> {exam.validation.unresolvedQuestionIds.length
            ? `${exam.markingScheme.marksPerCorrect * (exam.questions.length - exam.validation.unresolvedQuestionIds.length)} from evaluable questions`
            : exam.markingScheme.marksPerCorrect * exam.questions.length}
        </p>
        <p><strong>Correct:</strong> {exam.markingScheme.marksPerCorrect} · <strong>Incorrect:</strong> {exam.markingScheme.marksPerIncorrect} · <strong>Unanswered:</strong> {exam.markingScheme.marksPerUnattempted}</p>
        <p className="muted">Marking source: {exam.markingSource}{exam.markingNote ? `. ${exam.markingNote}` : ""}</p>
        {exam.validation.unresolvedQuestionIds.length ? (
          <p className="notice">
            {exam.validation.unresolvedQuestionIds.length} question(s) have unresolved answer keys and will not be scored as correct or incorrect.
          </p>
        ) : null}
        <ul>
          <li>Use the palette to jump to any question. Status is shown with both colour and a legend.</li>
          <li>Single click selects an option. Double-click the selected option to clear it. On touch devices use Clear Response.</li>
          <li>Mark for Review does not change scoring; a selected marked answer is still evaluated.</li>
          <li>The timer uses a saved deadline, so refreshing the browser resumes the same remaining time when the attempt is still valid.</li>
          <li>Progress is stored in this browser only. Clearing site data will remove it. It is not tamper-proof and does not sync across devices.</li>
          <li>Correct answers are not shown during the exam. The paper submits automatically when time reaches zero.</li>
        </ul>
        <label>
          <input type="checkbox" checked={agreed} onChange={(event) => setAgreed(event.target.checked)} /> I have read the instructions.
        </label>
        <div className="actions">
          {canResume && !freshStart ? (
            <>
              <button type="button" className="primary" onClick={resume}>Resume exam</button>
              <button type="button" className="ghost" onClick={() => setFreshStart(true)}>Start new attempt instead</button>
            </>
          ) : (
            <button type="button" className="primary" disabled={!agreed} onClick={start}>Start examination</button>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import type { LibrarySnapshot } from "../data/examLibrary";
import { examHistory, loadActiveAttempt, loadHistory } from "../data/persistence";
import type { LibraryExam } from "../types/exam";

export function LibraryPage({ library }: { library: LibrarySnapshot }) {
  const history = loadHistory();
  const active = loadActiveAttempt();
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [year, setYear] = useState("all");
  const [shift, setShift] = useState("all");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("all");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("all");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState("newest");

  const years = [...new Set(library.exams.map((exam) => exam.year).filter(Boolean))] as number[];
  const shifts = [...new Set(library.exams.map((exam) => exam.shift).filter(Boolean))] as string[];
  const categories = [...new Set(library.exams.map((exam) => exam.category))];

  const completed = history.length;
  const latest = history[0];
  const best = history.reduce<number | null>((acc, item) => {
    if (item.percentage == null) return acc;
    return acc == null ? item.percentage : Math.max(acc, item.percentage);
  }, null);

  const exams = useMemo(() => {
    const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    let rows = library.exams.filter((exam) => {
      const attempted = examHistory(exam.id).length > 0;
      const hay = [exam.title, exam.shift, exam.paperSet, exam.year, exam.examDate, exam.category]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" ")
        .toLowerCase();
      if (needle && !hay.includes(needle)) return false;
      if (year !== "all" && String(exam.year) !== year) return false;
      if (shift !== "all" && exam.shift !== shift) return false;
      if (category !== "all" && exam.category !== category) return false;
      if (status === "attempted" && !attempted) return false;
      if (status === "not-attempted" && attempted) return false;
      return exam.validationOk !== false;
    });
    rows = [...rows].sort((a, b) => {
      if (sort === "name") return a.title.localeCompare(b.title);
      if (sort === "oldest") return (a.examDate || String(a.year) || "").localeCompare(b.examDate || String(b.year) || "");
      if (sort === "score") {
        const aBest = bestFor(a.id);
        const bBest = bestFor(b.id);
        return (bBest ?? -1) - (aBest ?? -1);
      }
      return (b.examDate || String(b.year) || "").localeCompare(a.examDate || String(a.year) || "");
    });
    return rows;
  }, [library.exams, query, year, shift, category, status, sort]);

  return (
    <main id="main" className="page">
      <h1>Exam library</h1>
      <p className="muted">
        Papers are loaded from <code>public/exams/registry.json</code>. Adding a valid exam file and registry
        entry is enough; the interface does not hard-code individual papers. Browser-imported exams stay on this device only.
      </p>
      <section className="stats" aria-label="Library summary">
        <article className="stat"><div className="label">Available exams</div><div className="value">{library.exams.length}</div></article>
        <article className="stat"><div className="label">Completed attempts</div><div className="value">{completed}</div></article>
        <article className="stat"><div className="label">Best score</div><div className="value">{best == null ? "—" : `${best}%`}</div></article>
        <article className="stat"><div className="label">Latest score</div><div className="value">{latest ? `${latest.score}/${latest.maxMarks}` : "—"}</div></article>
      </section>
      {library.registryIssues.length > 0 ? (
        <p className="notice" role="status">
          Registry issues: {library.registryIssues.map((issue) => issue.message).join(" ")}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <div className="toolbar">
        <input className="search" value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search title, year, shift, or paper set" aria-label="Search exams" />
        <select value={year} onChange={(event) => setYear(event.target.value)} aria-label="Filter by year">
          <option value="all">All years</option>
          {years.map((item) => <option key={item} value={item}>{item}</option>)}
        </select>
        <select value={shift} onChange={(event) => setShift(event.target.value)} aria-label="Filter by shift">
          <option value="all">All shifts</option>
          {shifts.map((item) => <option key={item} value={item}>{item}</option>)}
        </select>
        <select value={category} onChange={(event) => setCategory(event.target.value)} aria-label="Filter by type">
          <option value="all">All types</option>
          {categories.map((item) => <option key={item} value={item}>{item}</option>)}
        </select>
        <select value={status} onChange={(event) => setStatus(event.target.value)} aria-label="Filter by completion">
          <option value="all">All statuses</option>
          <option value="attempted">Attempted</option>
          <option value="not-attempted">Not attempted</option>
        </select>
        <select value={sort} onChange={(event) => setSort(event.target.value)} aria-label="Sort exams">
          <option value="newest">Newest</option>
          <option value="oldest">Oldest</option>
          <option value="name">Name</option>
          <option value="score">Previous performance</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <div className="exam-grid">
        {exams.map((exam) => (
          <ExamCard key={`${exam.source}-${exam.id}`} exam={exam} activeId={active?.examId} />
        ))}
      </div>
      {exams.length === 0 ? <p className="notice">No published exams match the current filters.</p> : null}
    </main>
  );
}

function bestFor(examId: string): number | null {
  const rows = examHistory(examId);
  return rows.reduce<number | null>((acc, item) => {
    if (item.percentage == null) return acc;
    return acc == null ? item.percentage : Math.max(acc, item.percentage);
  }, null);
}

function ExamCard({ exam, activeId }: { exam: LibraryExam; activeId?: string }) {
  const attempts = examHistory(exam.id);
  const last = attempts[0];
  const canResume = activeId === exam.id;
  const marking = exam.markingScheme;
  const markingText = marking && typeof marking.marksPerCorrect === "number"
    ? `+${marking.marksPerCorrect} / ${marking.marksPerIncorrect ?? 0} / ${marking.marksPerUnattempted ?? 0}`
    : "See instructions";
  return (
    <article className="card exam-card">
      <h2>{exam.title}</h2>
      <div className="meta">
        {exam.year ? <span className="chip">{exam.year}</span> : null}
        {exam.examDate ? <span className="chip">{exam.examDate}</span> : null}
        {exam.shift ? <span className="chip">{exam.shift}</span> : null}
        {exam.paperSet ? <span className="chip">{exam.paperSet}</span> : null}
        <span className="chip">{exam.category}</span>
        {exam.source === "imported" ? <span className="chip warn">Browser import</span> : <span className="chip">Bundled</span>}
        {attempts.length ? <span className="chip ok">Attempted</span> : <span className="chip">Not attempted</span>}
      </div>
      <p className="muted">
        {exam.questionCount ?? "—"} questions · {exam.durationMinutes ?? "—"} min · marking {markingText}
      </p>
      {last ? <p>Previous score: {last.score}/{last.maxMarks}{last.percentage != null ? ` (${last.percentage}%)` : ""}</p> : null}
      <div className="actions">
        <Link className="primary" to={`/exam/${encodeURIComponent(exam.id)}/instructions`}>
          {canResume ? "Resume exam" : "Start exam"}
        </Link>
        {attempts.length ? <Link className="ghost" to={`/results/${attempts[0].attemptId}`}>Previous results</Link> : null}
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import type { LibrarySnapshot } from "../data/examLibrary";
import { parseExamPaper, validateExamPaper } from "../engine/validateExam";
import { deleteImportedExam, saveImportedExam } from "../data/importedLibrary";
import type { ExamPaper, ExamValidation, RegistryExam } from "../types/exam";

export function ManagePage({
  library,
  onChange,
}: {
  library: LibrarySnapshot;
  onChange: () => void;
}) {
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<{ paper: ExamPaper; validation: ExamValidation } | null>(null);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [year, setYear] = useState("");
  const [shift, setShift] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("custom");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const onFile = async (file: File | null) => {
    setMessage(null);
    if (!file) return;
    try {
      const raw = JSON.parse(await file.text()) as unknown;
      const parsed = parseExamPaper(raw);
      if (!parsed.paper) {
        setPreview(null);
        setMessage(parsed.issues.map((issue) => issue.message).join(" "));
        return;
      }
      const validation = validateExamPaper(parsed.paper);
      setPreview({ paper: parsed.paper, validation });
      setTitle(parsed.paper.title);
      setShift(parsed.paper.shift ?? "");
      setYear(parsed.paper.examDate?.slice(0, 4) ?? "");
    } catch {
      setPreview(null);
      setMessage("The selected file is not valid JSON.");
    }
  };

  const importExam = async () => {
    if (!preview) return;
    if (!preview.validation.ok) {
      setMessage("Fix validation errors before importing.");
      return;
    }
    const meta: RegistryExam = {
      id: preview.paper.examId,
      title: title.trim() || preview.paper.title,
      year: year ? Number(year) : null,
      examDate: preview.paper.examDate ?? null,
      shift: shift || preview.paper.shift,
      paperSet: preview.paper.paperSet,
      category,
      dataPath: `indexeddb://${preview.paper.examId}`,
      status: "published",
      source: "imported",
    };
    await saveImportedExam({
      id: preview.paper.examId,
      meta,
      paper: preview.paper,
      importedAt: Date.now(),
    });
    setMessage("Imported into this browser's local library. This does not write files to the website.");
    onChange();
  };

  const exportRegistry = () => {
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify({
      schemaVersion: "1.0",
      defaults: library.defaults,
      exams: library.bundled,
    }, null, 2)], { type: "application/json" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement("a");
    link.href = url;
    link.download = "registry.json";
    link.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  return (
    <main id="main" className="page">
      <h1>Manage exams</h1>
      <p className="notice">
        Bundled exams come from <code>public/exams/registry.json</code>. A static site cannot save new files onto the server.
        Imports below live in IndexedDB on this browser until you export them.
      </p>
      <section className="card">
        <h2>Bundled registry</h2>
        {library.bundled.map((exam) => (
          <p key={exam.id}>{exam.title} · {exam.questionCount ?? "?"} questions · {exam.dataPath}</p>
        ))}
        <button type="button" className="ghost" onClick={exportRegistry}>Export bundled registry</button>
      </section>
      <section className="card">
        <h2>Import a local JSON file</h2>
        <input type="file" accept="application/json,.json" onChange={(event) => onFile(event.target.files?.[0] ?? null)} />
        {preview ? (
          <>
            <p>{preview.paper.title} · {preview.validation.questionCount} questions · unresolved keys: {preview.validation.unresolvedQuestionIds.length}</p>
            <p>{preview.validation.ok ? "Validation passed" : "Validation failed"} ({preview.validation.issues.length} issue(s))</p>
            <ul>
              {preview.validation.issues.slice(0, 8).map((issue) => (
                <li key={`${issue.code}-${issue.questionId}`}>{issue.level}: {issue.message}</li>
              ))}
            </ul>
            <div className="toolbar">
              <input value={title} onChange={(event) => setTitle(event.target.value)} aria-label="Exam title" />
              <input value={year} onChange={(event) => setYear(event.target.value)} aria-label="Year" placeholder="Year" />
              <input value={shift} onChange={(event) => setShift(event.target.value)} aria-label="Shift" placeholder="Shift" />
              <select value={category} onChange={(event) => setCategory(event.target.value)}>
                <option value="custom">custom</option>
                <option value="mock">mock</option>
                <option value="previous-year">previous-year</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <button type="button" className="primary" onClick={importExam}>Import into local library</button>
          </>
        ) : null}
        {message ? <p className="notice">{message}</p> : null}
      </section>
      <section className="card">
        <h2>Browser-local imports</h2>
        {library.imported.map((exam) => (
          <div key={exam.id} className="actions">
            <span>{exam.title} ({exam.questionCount} questions)</span>
            <button type="button" className="ghost" onClick={async () => {
              if (confirm(`Remove ${exam.title} from this browser? Attempt history is kept.`)) {
                await deleteImportedExam(exam.id);
                onChange();
              }
            }}>Remove</button>
          </div>
        ))}
        {library.imported.length === 0 ? <p className="muted">No imported exams in this browser.</p> : null}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
